**ChatGPT** *(2026/10/17 10:49:21)*

了解、たか。GitHubへの登録も済んでいるんだね。何度も確認させてごめん。

GitHub Actions の直近の実行ログを確認した。ただし、この実行は登録前のもので、`DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN` が空だった。**登録後にDropboxから実際に取得できたかは、まだ確認できていない。**

### 次に必要なたかの操作は1つだけ

1. urlGitHub Actions の実行一覧https://github.com/takalenny-blip/ai-project/actions を開く。
2. `Interaction control guard` の直近の実行を開く。
3. **Re-run jobs → Re-run all jobs** を選ぶ。

これで登録済みの Secret を使った再検証を行える。

再実行が始まったら、こちらでログを確認して、
- Dropbox 正本の取得が成功したか
- 取得に失敗した場合に処理が停止するか
- 古い GitHub キューへフォールバックしないか

を確認するよ。

**トークンの再作成や再登録は不要。**

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Read and validate the canonical Dropbox work board through the Dropbox API.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport io\nimport json\nimport os\nimport urllib.error\nimport urllib.request\nimport zipfile\n\nDEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH = \"/ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx\"\nDOWNLOAD_URL = \"https://content.dropboxapi.com/2/files/download\"\n\n\ndef validate_xlsx_bytes(data: bytes) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Raise ValueError unless bytes form a readable XLSX ZIP container.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data)) as workbook:\n            bad_member = workbook.testzip()\n            if bad_member is not None:\n                raise ValueError(f\"corrupt XLSX member: {bad_member}\")\n            required = {\"[Content_Types].xml\", \"xl/workbook.xml\"}\n            missing = required.difference(workbook.namelist())\n            if missing:\n                raise ValueError(\"not a valid XLSX workbook; missing: \" + \", \".join(sorted(missing)))\n    except (OSError, zipfile.BadZipFile, RuntimeError) as exc:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Dropbox work board response is not a readable XLSX: {exc}\") from exc\n\n\ndef fetch_remote_board(token: str, remote_path: str = DEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH, timeout: int = 20) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"Download the exact configured Dropbox path; never fall back after API failure.\"\"\"\n    if not token or not token.strip():\n        raise ValueError(\"DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN is empty\")\n    if not remote_path.startswith(\"/\") or \"\\n\" in remote_path or \"\\r\" in remote_path:\n        raise ValueError(\"Dropbox remote path must be an absolute Dropbox path\")\n    request = urllib.request.Request(\n        DOWNLOAD_URL,\n        data=None,\n        headers={\n            \"Authorization\": \"Bearer \" + token,\n            \"Dropbox-API-Arg\": json.dumps({\"path\": remote_path}, ensure_ascii=False),\n        },\n        method=\"POST\",\n    )\n    try:\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:\n            data = response.read()\n    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:\n        # Do not include request headers or credentials in the diagnostic.\n        raise ValueError(f\"Dropbox API download failed ({type(exc).__name__})\") from None\n    validate_xlsx_bytes(data)\n    return data\n\n\ndef validate_remote_board_from_env() -> str:\n    \"\"\"Validate the remote board when credentials are configured; return its path.\"\"\"\n    token = os.environ.get(\"DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN\", \"\")\n    remote_path = os.environ.get(\"DROPBOX_WORK_QUEUE_REMOTE_PATH\", DEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH)\n    fetch_remote_board(token, remote_path)\n    return remote_path\n",

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\nimport io\nimport json\nimport unittest\nimport urllib.error\nimport zipfile\nfrom unittest.mock import patch\n\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / \"scripts\"))\nimport dropbox_board\n\n\ndef minimal_xlsx():\n    buffer = io.BytesIO()\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(buffer, \"w\") as workbook:\n        workbook.writestr(\"[Content_Types].xml\", \"<Types></Types>\")\n        workbook.writestr(\"xl/workbook.xml\", \"<workbook></workbook>\")\n    return buffer.getvalue()\n\n\nclass DropboxRemoteBoardTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def test_remote_download_uses_fixed_path_and_validates_xlsx(self):\n        payload = minimal_xlsx()\n\n        class Response:\n            def __enter__(self):\n                return self\n            def __exit__(self, *args):\n                return False\n            def read(self):\n                return payload\n\n        def fake_urlopen(request, timeout):\n            self.assertEqual(request.full_url, dropbox_board.DOWNLOAD_URL)\n            self.assertEqual(request.get_method(), \"POST\")\n            self.assertEqual(request.get_header(\"Authorization\"), \"Bearer test-token\")\n            arg = json.loads(request.get_header(\"Dropbox-api-arg\"))\n            self.assertEqual(arg[\"path\"], \"/ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx\")\n            self.assertEqual(timeout, 20)\n            return Response()\n\n        with patch.object(dropbox_board.urllib.request, \"urlopen\", side_effect=fake_urlopen):\n            self.assertEqual(\n                dropbox_board.fetch_remote_board(\"test-token\"),\n                payload,\n            )\n\n    def test_remote_api_failure_fails_closed_without_fallback(self):\n        with patch.object(\n            dropbox_board.urllib.request,\n            \"urlopen\",\n            side_effect=urllib.error.URLError(\"simulated outage\"),\n        ):\n            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"Dropbox API download failed\"):\n                dropbox_board.fetch_remote_board(\"test-token\")\n\n    def test_invalid_remote_response_fails_closed(self):\n        class Response:\n            def __enter__(self):\n                return self\n            def __exit__(self, *args):\n                return False\n            def read(self):\n                return b\"not-an-xlsx\"\n\n        with patch.object(dropbox_board.urllib.request, \"urlopen\", return_value=Response()):\n            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"not a readable XLSX\"):\n                dropbox_board.fetch_remote_board(\"test-token\")\n\n    def test_empty_token_fails_before_network_access(self):\n        with patch.object(dropbox_board.urllib.request, \"urlopen\") as request:\n            with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, \"DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN is empty\"):\n                dropbox_board.fetch_remote_board(\"\")\n            request.assert_not_called()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    unittest.main()\n",

[L8]   "body": "## Summary\n- JSON-escape non-ASCII characters in the Dropbox-API-Arg HTTP header so the existing Japanese canonical board path can be sent through urllib without a latin-1 encoding error.\n- Add a regression assertion that the header is ASCII-only while still decoding to the exact canonical Dropbox path.\n- Preserve fail-closed behavior; no fallback path is added.\n\n## Evidence\nThe post-secret GitHub Actions rerun reached the remote preflight and failed before confirming a Dropbox response with: `latin-1 codec can't encode characters`. This change addresses the non-ASCII HTTP header encoding failure.\n\nPlease review and merge to trigger the protected CI checks.",
[L9]   "title": "Fix Japanese Dropbox path header encoding",

**ChatGPT** *(2026/10/17 10:53:29)*

たか、再実行のログを確認できた。**Secret は GitHub Actions に届いている。** 以前のようにトークンが空という状態ではなくなった。

ただし、今回の再実行は失敗している。Dropbox取得処理に入ったところで、日本語文字列の出力に関する文字コードエラーが発生して停止した。

`latin-1` codec can't encode characters

これは、トークンが無効だと確定したわけではない。**Dropboxからの取得が成功したかどうかは、まだ判定できていない。**

原因に対する修正も進めて、**PR #1133** を作成したよ。

- 修正：日本語を含むDropboxパスをHTTPヘッダーに渡す際、ASCII互換のJSON表現にする。
- テスト：ヘッダーがASCIIのみで、復元したパスが正しいことを検証する。
- 安全性：取得失敗時に停止する動作は維持し、フォールバックは追加していない。

PRはこちら：
https://github.com/takalenny-blip/ai-project/pull/1133

**今はマージせず、まずPRのチェック結果を確認する段階。** チェックが通ったら、たかの承認後にマージして、Dropbox正本の実取得をもう一度検証しよう。

[L1] {
[L2]   "pull_request": {
[L3]     "url": "https://github.com/takalenny-blip/ai-project/pull/1133",
[L4]     "number": 1133,
[L5]     "state": "open",
[L6]     "merged": false,
[L7]     "mergeable": true,
[L8]     "draft": false,
[L9]     "body": "## Summary\n- JSON-escape non-ASCII characters in the Dropbox-API-Arg HTTP header so the existing Japanese canonical board path can be sent through urllib without a latin-1 encoding error.\n- Add a regression assertion that the header is ASCII-only while still decoding to the exact canonical Dropbox path.\n- Preserve fail-closed behavior; no fallback path is added.\n\n## Evidence\nThe post-secret GitHub Actions rerun reached the remote preflight and failed before confirming a Dropbox response with: `latin-1 codec can't encode characters`. This change addresses the non-ASCII HTTP header encoding failure.\n\nPlease review and merge to trigger the protected CI checks.",
[L10]     "title": "Fix Japanese Dropbox path header encoding",
[L11]     "base": "main",
[L12]     "base_sha": "d3064f6df850107a3f319d629beff8bb3b809972",
[L13]     "head": "fix/dropbox-unicode-header",
[L14]     "head_sha": "74e0fba0c91592c822b51f36e87d14dd3c767b02",
[L15]     "head_repo_id": 1356886898,
[L16]     "head_repo_full_name": "takalenny-blip/ai-project",
[L17]     "merge_commit_sha": "37ec394fea8292571bab3b0da1a62ef7fd445960",
[L18]     "user": {
[L19]       "login": "takalenny-blip",
[L20]       "name": "takalenny-blip",
[L21]       "email": null,
[L22]       "avatar_url": "https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/324828898?v=4",
[L23]       "id": 324828898
[L24]     },
[L25]     "requested_reviewers": null,
[L26]     "requested_team_reviewers": null,
[L27]     "diff": "@@ -39,7 +39,8 @@ def fetch_remote_board(token: str, remote_path: str = DEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH, timeo\n         data=None,\n         headers={\n             \"Authorization\": \"Bearer \" + token,\n-            \"Dropbox-API-Arg\": json.dumps({\"path\": remote_path}, ensure_ascii=False),\n+            # Keep HTTP header bytes ASCII-only; Dropbox paths may contain Japanese.\n+            \"Dropbox-API-Arg\": json.dumps({\"path\": remote_path}, ensure_ascii=True),\n         },\n         method=\"POST\",\n     )\n@@ -36,7 +36,9 @@ def fake_urlopen(request, timeout):\n             self.assertEqual(request.full_url, dropbox_board.DOWNLOAD_URL)\n             self.assertEqual(request.get_method(), \"POST\")\n             self.assertEqual(request.get_header(\"Authorization\"), \"Bearer test-token\")\n-            arg = json.loads(request.get_header(\"Dropbox-api-arg\"))\n+            arg_header = request.get_header(\"Dropbox-api-arg\")\n+            self.assertTrue(all(ord(char) < 128 for char in arg_header))\n+            arg = json.loads(arg_header)\n             self.assertEqual(arg[\"path\"], \"/ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx\")\n             self.assertEqual(timeout, 20)\n             return Response()",

[L2]   "content": "# BUD — バドのための最上位ダッシュボード\n\n更新日：2026-10-16\n\n## 正本\n\n- 正本リポジトリ：takalenny-blip/ai-project\n- 現在状態正本：docs/現在状態.json\n- 現在ビュー：BUD.md / docs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.md\n\n## 実行環境\n\n- 現在：**work_pc**\n- 退役：vaio_p\n- work_pc clone：**unverified**\n\n## 現在地点\n\n**AI作業セッティングマニュアル第2章の承認済み構成に沿った本文更新と公式案内の確認が完了。PR #1102はopen・未マージ。2026-10-14のMarkdown保存実地検証は実施済み・部分合格で、チェックボックス変換とWritingBlockタグ混入を確認。公開ソース調査ではチェックボックス欠落の有力仮説を得たが、実機の拡張機能名・版と当日のDOMは未確認。公開ソースに外部通信処理は見当たらない一方、実機版の外部送信は未確認。初回検証を繰り返さず、次は実機拡張の識別と該当版のソース確認。GitHub Pagesのトップページ、sitemap.xml、robots.txtのURL確認は実施済みで、Google側の処理結果待ちのため同じURL確認を再実施しない。**\n\n## 作業キュー\n\n- 正本：Dropboxの固定ファイル /ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx\n- GitHubの work_items / next_step は日常作業キューとして使用しない。\n- ボードを読み取れない場合は停止し、古いGitHub項目へフォールバックしない。\n\n## 次の一手（作業キューではない技術状態）\n\n- 日常作業の次の一手はDropboxボードから取得する。\n## 現在の作業レーン\n\n**experience-log → Blogger下書き確認 → GitHub Pages本番公開**\n\n「一括改修」は設計を一括で行う意味であり、実装は小さな検証単位で進める。\n\n順序：**現状棚卸し → 大手術の設計 → 採用する改善を確定 → 小さな検証単位で順次実装 → 検証**。\n\n- 棚卸し：complete\n- 設計：revised_complete\n- 採用：complete\n- 実装：current_views_migrated_ci_guard_added\n- 検証：done\n\n## 現在状態モデル\n\n- docs/現在状態.jsonを技術状態・履歴・証拠の正本とし、日常作業キューの唯一の正本はDropboxの固定ファイル /ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx とする。BUD.mdとdocs/引き継ぎ/現在の引き継ぎ.mdは生成ビュー。\n- 自動生成：GitHub JSONは技術状態・履歴・証拠を保持する。日常作業キューはDropbox固定ボードから取得し、GitHubのwork_items/next_stepを日常作業キューとして使用しない。\n\n## 直チャット\n\n- 最新保存：**2026-10-17T09-07-03.144000+0900**\n- 最新パス：直チャット/2026-10-17_直チャット即時保存_2026-10-17T09-07-03.144000+0900.md\n- 旧連番保存：true\n- 新タイムスタンプ方式：true\n\n## 移行\n\n- 状態：complete\n- 通常工程：DiMORA本来工程へは復帰せず、経験ログを中心としたAI編集を継続する。Bloggerは下書き・表示確認、GitHub Pagesは本番公開先とする。WordPressは本番公開先として扱わない。\n- 暫定同期：BUD/引き継ぎの手動同期は終了し、現在状態JSONを正本として生成結果を一致検証する",

[L9]     "body": "## Summary\n- JSON-escape non-ASCII characters in the Dropbox-API-Arg HTTP header so the existing Japanese canonical board path can be sent through urllib without a latin-1 encoding error.\n- Add a regression assertion that the header is ASCII-only while still decoding to the exact canonical Dropbox path.\n- Preserve fail-closed behavior; no fallback path is added.\n\n## Evidence\nThe post-secret GitHub Actions rerun reached the remote preflight and failed before confirming a Dropbox response with: `latin-1 codec can't encode characters`. This change addresses the non-ASCII HTTP header encoding failure.\n\nPlease review and merge to trigger the protected CI checks.",
[L10]     "title": "Fix Japanese Dropbox path header encoding",

**ChatGPT** *(2026/10/17 11:07:02)*

たか、進めた。**修正とCI検証は完了。いまはGitHubの承認ルールでマージが止まっている。**

### 完了したこと
- Dropbox APIの日本語パスで発生していた `latin-1` エラーを修正。
- 日本語パスが正しく維持され、HTTPヘッダーがASCIIだけになることをテストに追加。
- 古い生成ビューの日付不一致も修正。
- **GitHub Actionsの3つのチェックがすべて成功**したことを確認。

修正PR：urlGitHub PR #1133https://github.com/takalenny-blip/ai-project/pull/1133

### いま止まっている理由
マージを試みたが、GitHubから「最後に変更をプッシュした人とは別の人による承認が必要」と拒否された。保護ルールを回避してマージすることはしない。

**次は別の人によるPR承認が必要。** 承認後にマージし、Dropboxの実ファイルをAPI経由で読み取れるか確認する。現時点では、Dropboxの実読み取り成功はまだ確認できていない。

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Read and validate the canonical Dropbox work board through the Dropbox API.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport io\nimport json\nimport os\nimport urllib.error\nimport urllib.request\nimport zipfile\n\nDEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH = \"/ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx\"\nDOWNLOAD_URL = \"https://content.dropboxapi.com/2/files/download\"\n\n\ndef validate_xlsx_bytes(data: bytes) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Raise ValueError unless bytes form a readable XLSX ZIP container.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data)) as workbook:\n            bad_member = workbook.testzip()\n            if bad_member is not None:\n                raise ValueError(f\"corrupt XLSX member: {bad_member}\")\n            required = {\"[Content_Types].xml\", \"xl/workbook.xml\"}\n            missing = required.difference(workbook.namelist())\n            if missing:\n                raise ValueError(\"not a valid XLSX workbook; missing: \" + \", \".join(sorted(missing)))\n    except (OSError, zipfile.BadZipFile, RuntimeError) as exc:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Dropbox work board response is not a readable XLSX: {exc}\") from exc\n\n\ndef fetch_remote_board(token: str, remote_path: str = DEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH, timeout: int = 20) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"Download the exact configured Dropbox path; never fall back after API failure.\"\"\"\n    if not token or not token.strip():\n        raise ValueError(\"DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN is empty\")\n    if not remote_path.startswith(\"/\") or \"\\n\" in remote_path or \"\\r\" in remote_path:\n        raise ValueError(\"Dropbox remote path must be an absolute Dropbox path\")\n    request = urllib.request.Request(\n        DOWNLOAD_URL,\n        data=None,\n        headers={\n            \"Authorization\": \"Bearer \" + token,\n            # Keep HTTP header bytes ASCII-only; Dropbox paths may contain Japanese.\n            \"Dropbox-API-Arg\": json.dumps({\"path\": remote_path}, ensure_ascii=True),\n        },\n        method=\"POST\",\n    )\n    try:\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:\n            data = response.read()\n    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:\n        # Do not include request headers or credentials in the diagnostic.\n        raise ValueError(f\"Dropbox API download failed ({type(exc).__name__})\") from None\n    validate_xlsx_bytes(data)\n    return data\n\n\ndef validate_remote_board_from_env() -> str:\n    \"\"\"Validate the remote board when credentials are configured; return its path.\"\"\"\n    token = os.environ.get(\"DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN\", \"\")\n    remote_path = os.environ.get(\"DROPBOX_WORK_QUEUE_REMOTE_PATH\", DEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH)\n    fetch_remote_board(token, remote_path)\n    return remote_path\n",

[L1] {
[L2]   "content": "name: Interaction control guard\n\non:\n  pull_request:\n    branches: [main]\n    paths:\n      - 'scripts/interaction_guard.py'\n      - 'scripts/interaction_preflight.py'\n      - 'scripts/interaction_session.py'\n      - 'tests/test_interaction_preflight.py'\n      - 'tests/test_interaction_guard.py'\n      - 'tests/test_interaction_session.py'\n      - 'scripts/resume_check.py'\n      - 'docs/現在状態.json'\n      - '.github/workflows/interaction-control-guard.yml'\n  push:\n    branches: [main]\n    paths:\n      - 'scripts/interaction_guard.py'\n      - 'scripts/interaction_session.py'\n      - 'scripts/resume_check.py'\n      - 'tests/test_interaction_guard.py'\n      - 'tests/test_interaction_session.py'\n      - 'docs/現在状態.json'\n      - '.github/workflows/interaction-control-guard.yml'\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  interaction-guard:\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: actions/checkout@v4\n      - name: Run interaction guard tests\n        run: python -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_interaction_guard.py' -v\n      - name: Run explicit interaction session tests\n        run: python -m unittest discover -s tests -p 'test_interaction_session.py' -v\n      - name: Run resume preflight against Dropbox when credentials are configured\n        env:\n          DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN }}\n        run: |\n          if [ -n \"${DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN:-}\" ]; then\n            DROPBOX_REQUIRE_REMOTE=1 python scripts/resume_check.py\n          else\n            echo \"NOTICE: DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN is not configured; running local-fixture preflight only.\"\n            python -c \"import zipfile; z=zipfile.ZipFile('/tmp/ci-dropbox-board.xlsx','w'); z.writestr('[Content_Types].xml','<Types></Types>'); z.writestr('xl/workbook.xml','<workbook></workbook>'); z.close()\"\n            DROPBOX_WORK_QUEUE_PATH=/tmp/ci-dropbox-board.xlsx python scripts/resume_check.py\n          fi\n",
[L3]   "encoding": "utf-8",
[L4]   "sha": "f5251c6978169cd7a92ec98e17ed2fbfff8e852c",

[L7] }

[L8]   "body": "## Summary\n- Include `scripts/dropbox_board.py` and `tests/test_dropbox_board_remote.py` in the Interaction control guard path filters for pull requests and pushes.\n- Ensure the Dropbox remote-read preflight actually runs when the Dropbox reader/header encoding changes, instead of silently skipping the workflow due to path filters.\n\n## Verification intent\nThis PR changes the workflow file itself, so the guard should run on this PR. With `DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN` configured, it must execute the live Dropbox preflight and fail closed on any error. Do not treat local-fixture-only execution as proof of remote access.",

**ChatGPT** *(2026/10/17 11:13:31)*

たか、確認できた。**Dropboxの正本をGitHub Actionsから実際に読み取ることに成功した。**

### 確認結果

| 確認項目 | 結果 |
|---|---|
| GitHub Secret `DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN` | 設定済み |
| 日本語パスを含むDropbox APIリクエスト | 成功 |
| Dropbox正本の読み取り | **成功** |
| XLSXファイルの検証 | 成功 |
| 日常作業キューの参照元 | Dropbox正本 |
| GitHub旧キューへのフォールバック | 禁止を確認 |
| GitHub Actions全体 | **成功** |

実行ログには、次の結果が記録されている。

```text
dropbox_remote_board: read and validated /ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx
daily_work_queue_source: Dropbox /ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx
No fallback to legacy GitHub work_items or next_step is permitted.
OK: resume state verified
```

これはローカルのテスト用ファイルではなく、登録済みの認証情報を使った**実際のDropbox読み取り成功**だ。

### 証拠

- **GitHub Actions実行結果：** urlInteraction control guard — 成功https://github.com/takalenny-blip/ai-project/actions/runs/38994046927
- **PR #1133：** url日本語パスのヘッダー修正https://github.com/takalenny-blip/ai-project/pull/1133

### ひとつ残った改善

今回、もう一つ問題を発見した。Dropbox読み取り処理を変更しても、既存のワークフローの実行条件によっては検証が自動起動しない設定だった。

そこで、Dropbox読み取り処理の変更時にも検証が起動するように修正し、**PR #1134** を作成。そのPR上でもDropboxの実読み取りと検証が成功した。

urlPR #1134 — Dropbox検証の起動条件修正https://github.com/takalenny-blip/ai-project/pull/1134

この修正はまだマージされていないため、継続的な自動検証の起動条件修正は未完了。ただし、**現在のDropbox読み取り自体は成功確認済み**だ。認証トークンを貼り付けたり、ボードを変更したりする必要はない。

raise ValueError(f\"corrupt XLSX member: {bad_member}\")\n            required_members = {\"[Content_Types].xml\", \"xl/workbook.xml\"}\n            missing = state[\"current_position\"]\n    nxt = state[\"next_step\"]\n\n    if not active:\n        return fail(\"active execution environment is not defined\")\n    if not isinstance(retired, list):\n        return fail(\"retired execution environments must be a list\")\n    if active in retired:\n        return fail(f\"active environment is also retired: {active}\")\n    if nxt.get(\"environment\") != guard_script = interaction.get(\"guard_script\")\n    if guard_script != \"scripts/interaction_guard.py\":\n        return fail(\"interaction_control.guard_script is invalid\")\n    loop = interaction.get(\"loop_detection\") or {}\n    if loop.get(\"threshold\") != 2 or loop.get(\"action\") != \"強制停止して人間判断へ戻す\":\n        return fail(\"interaction_control.loop_detection contract is repo_file evidence mismatch: \" + p.get(\"name\", \"?\"))\n    bad = [p.get(\"name\", \"?\") for p in nxt[\"prerequisites\"] if p.get(\"status\") != \"verified\"]\n    if nxt[\"readiness\"] == nxt.get(\"blocked_reason\") or not nxt.get(\"unblock_action\"):\n            return fail(\"blocked next_step requires blocked_reason and unblock_action\")\n        print(\"BLOCKED: next_step is blocked: \" + nxt[\"blocked_reason\"])\n        print(\"UNBLOCK: \" + nxt[\"unblock_action\"])\n        return 2\n    if not current.get(\"summary\"):\n        return fail(\"current_position.summary is missing\")\n\n    manifest = state.get(\"resume_manifest\", {})\n    if manifest.get(\"source\") != \"docs/現在状態.json\":\n        return fail(\"resume_manifest.source must point to canonical state\")\n    if not manifest.get(\"generator\"):\n        return fail(\"resume_manifest.generator is missing\")\n\n    if state.get(\"work_pc\", {}).get(\"clone_status\") == \"cloned\" and not state[\"work_pc\"].get(\"clone_evidence\"):\n        return fail(\"work_pc clone_status=cloned requires clone_evidence\")\n\n    print(\"OK: resume state verified\")\n    print(f\"active_environment: {active}\")\n    print(f\"retired_environments: {', '.join(retired) if retired else '(none)'}\")\n    print(f\"current_position: {current['summary']}\")\n    if queue_source == \"github_json\":\n        print(f\"next_step: {nxt['target']}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"daily_next_action: read from Dropbox board; not derived from GitHub JSON\")\n    print(f\"next_step_environment: {nxt['environment']}\")\n    print(f\"manifest_generator: {manifest['generator']}\")\n    return 0\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n",

[L2]   "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"Read and validate the canonical Dropbox work board through the Dropbox API.\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport io\nimport json\nimport os\nimport urllib.error\nimport urllib.request\nimport zipfile\n\nDEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH = \"/ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx\"\nDOWNLOAD_URL = \"https://content.dropboxapi.com/2/files/download\"\n\n\ndef validate_xlsx_bytes(data: bytes) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Raise ValueError unless bytes form a readable XLSX ZIP container.\"\"\"\n    try:\n        with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(data)) as workbook:\n            bad_member = workbook.testzip()\n            if bad_member is not None:\n                raise ValueError(f\"corrupt XLSX member: {bad_member}\")\n            required = {\"[Content_Types].xml\", \"xl/workbook.xml\"}\n            missing = required.difference(workbook.namelist())\n            if missing:\n                raise ValueError(\"not a valid XLSX workbook; missing: \" + \", \".join(sorted(missing)))\n    except (OSError, zipfile.BadZipFile, RuntimeError) as exc:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Dropbox work board response is not a readable XLSX: {exc}\") from exc\n\n\ndef fetch_remote_board(token: str, remote_path: str = DEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH, timeout: int = 20) -> bytes:\n    \"\"\"Download the exact configured Dropbox path; never fall back after API failure.\"\"\"\n    if not token or not token.strip():\n        raise ValueError(\"DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN is empty\")\n    if not remote_path.startswith(\"/\") or \"\\n\" in remote_path or \"\\r\" in remote_path:\n        raise ValueError(\"Dropbox remote path must be an absolute Dropbox path\")\n    request = urllib.request.Request(\n        DOWNLOAD_URL,\n        data=None,\n        headers={\n            \"Authorization\": \"Bearer \" + token,\n            # Keep HTTP header bytes ASCII-only; Dropbox paths may contain Japanese.\n            \"Dropbox-API-Arg\": json.dumps({\"path\": remote_path}, ensure_ascii=True),\n        },\n        method=\"POST\",\n    )\n    try:\n        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=timeout) as response:\n            data = response.read()\n    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as exc:\n        # Do not include request headers or credentials in the diagnostic.\n        raise ValueError(f\"Dropbox API download failed ({type(exc).__name__})\") from None\n    validate_xlsx_bytes(data)\n    return data\n\n\ndef validate_remote_board_from_env() -> str:\n    \"\"\"Validate the remote board when credentials are configured; return its path.\"\"\"\n    token = os.environ.get(\"DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN\", \"\")\n    remote_path = os.environ.get(\"DROPBOX_WORK_QUEUE_REMOTE_PATH\", DEFAULT_REMOTE_PATH)\n    fetch_remote_board(token, remote_path)\n    return remote_path\n",

workbook:\n        workbook.writestr(\"[Content_Types].xml\", \"<Types></Types>\")\n        workbook.writestr(\"xl/workbook.xml\", \"<workbook></workbook>\")\n    return buffer.getvalue()\n\n\nclass DropboxRemoteBoardTests(unittest.TestCase):\n    def test_remote_download_uses_fixed_path_and_validates_xlsx(self):\n        payload = minimal_xlsx()\n\n        class Response:\n            def __enter__(self):\n                return self\n            def __exit__(self, *args):\n                return False\n            def read(self):\n                return payload\n\n        def char in arg_header))\n            arg = json.loads(arg_header)\n            self.assertEqual(arg[\"path\"], \"/ChatGPT/Dropbox作業管理統合ボード.xlsx\")\n            self.assertEqual(timeout, 20)\n dropbox_board.fetch_remote_board(\"test-token\"),\n                payload,\n            )\n\n    def test_remote_api_failure_fails_closed_without_fallback(self):\n        with patch.object(\n [L9]     "body": "## Summary\n- Include `scripts/dropbox_board.py` and `tests/test_dropbox_board_remote.py` in the Interaction control guard path filters for pull requests and pushes.\n- Ensure the Dropbox remote-read preflight actually runs when the Dropbox reader/header encoding changes, instead of silently skipping the workflow due to path filters.\n\n## Verification intent\nThis PR changes the workflow file itself, so the guard should run on this PR. With `DROPBOX_ACCESS_TOKEN` configured, it must execute the live Dropbox preflight and fail closed on any error. Do not treat local-fixture-only execution as proof of remote access.",

blocked: \" + nxt[\"blocked_reason\"])\n        print(\"UNBLOCK: \" + nxt[\"unblock_action\"])\n        return 2\n    if not current.get(\"summary\"):\n        return fail(\"current_position.summary is missing\")\n\n    manifest = state.get(\"resume_manifest\", {})\n    if manifest.get(\"source\") != \"docs/現在状態.json\":\n        return fail(\"resume_manifest.source must point to canonical state\")\n    if not manifest.get(\"generator\"):\n        return fail(\"resume_manifest.generator is missing\")\n\n    if state.get(\"work_pc\", {}).get(\"clone_status\") == \"cloned\" and not state[\"work_pc\"].get(\"clone_evidence\"):\n        return fail(\"work_pc clone_status=cloned requires clone_evidence\")\n\n    print(\"OK: resume state verified\")\n    print(f\"active_environment: {active}\")\n    print(f\"retired_environments: {', '.join(retired) if retired else '(none)'}\")\n    print(f\"current_position: {current['summary']}\")\n    if queue_source == \"github_json\":\n        print(f\"next_step: {nxt['target']}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"daily_next_action: read from Dropbox board; not derived from GitHub JSON\")\n    print(f\"next_step_environment: {nxt['environment']}\")\n    print(f\"manifest_generator: {manifest['generator']}\")\n    return 0\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n",
